-# transcendence
\ No newline at end of file
+# transcendence
+
+Para rodar no modo dev e o MinIO funcionar e necessario modificar o `/etc/hosts` e mapear também `127.0.0.1 <- videa.local`, assim sendo possivel acessar pelo navegador
+
+## No Windows
+### Abrir o arquivo hosts
+#### No Notepad (como administrador):
+- File → Open
+- navegar até:
+```bash
+C:\Windows\System32\drivers\etc\
+```
+- mudar o filtro de:
+```bash
+Text Documents (*.txt)
+```
+- para:
+```bash
+All Files (*.*)
+```
+- abrir o arquivo:
+```bash
+hosts
+```
+- Adicionar no final do arquivo:
+```bash
+127.0.0.1  videa.local api.videa.local s3.videa.local minio.videa.local
+```
+## No Linux
+
+- navegar até:
+```bash
+/etc/hosts
+```
+- abrir o arquivo:
+```bash
+hosts
+```
+- Adicionar no arquivo:
+```bash
+127.0.0.1  videa.local api.videa.local s3.videa.local minio.videa.local
+```
+#### Se necessario limpar o cache DNS
+
+- Linux: sudo systemd-resolve --flush-caches (ou equivalente)
+- macOS: sudo dscacheutil -flushcache
+- Windows: ipconfig /flushdns
+
+####  Se for rodar o modo prod localmente
+```bash
+GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI="http://videa.local"
+FRONTEND_ORIGIN="http://videa.local"
+
+VITE_BACKEND_URL="http://api.videa.local"
+FRONTEND_SERVER_NAME="videa.local"
+BACKEND_SERVER_NAME="api.videa.local"
+
+MINIO_ENDPOINT=minio.videa.local
+MINIO_API_ENDPOINT=s3.videa.local
+```
+E depois edite os servers names no .env
\ No newline at end of file

diff --git a/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/media/MediaValidationService.java b/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/media/MediaValidationService.java
new file mode 100644

+++ b/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/media/MediaValidationService.java
@@ -0,0 +1,169 @@
+package com.ft.transcendence.common.media;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
+import org.apache.tika.Tika;
+
+import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
+import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
+import java.io.InputStream;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.util.Set;
+
+@ApplicationScoped
+public class MediaValidationService {
+
+    private static final Set<String> VALID_IMAGE_TYPES = Set.of(
+            "image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif"
+    );
+
+    private static final Set<String> VALID_VIDEO_TYPES = Set.of(
+            "video/mp4",
+            "video/quicktime"
+    );
+
+    private static final long IMAGE_LIMIT = 5L * 1024 * 1024;
+    private static final long VIDEO_LIMIT = 20L * 1024 * 1024;
+
+    private final Tika tika = new Tika();
+    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();
+
+    public MediaAnalysis analyzeImage(Path path) throws Exception {
+        String mimeType = tika.detect(path);
+        return analyzeImage(path, mimeType);
+    }
+
+    public MediaAnalysis analyzeImage(Path path, String mimeType) throws Exception {
+
+        if (path == null || !Files.exists(path)) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "File not found", null, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        long size = Files.size(path);
+        if (size > IMAGE_LIMIT) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Image too large", null, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        if (!VALID_IMAGE_TYPES.contains(mimeType)) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Invalid image type", mimeType, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        if (!isValidImage(path)) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Invalid image content", mimeType, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        return new MediaAnalysis(true, null, mimeType, MediaKind.IMAGE);
+    }
+
+    public MediaAnalysis analyzeVideo(Path path, String mimeType) throws Exception {
+
+        if (path == null || !Files.exists(path)) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "File not found", null, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        long size = Files.size(path);
+        if (size > VIDEO_LIMIT) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Video too large", null, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        if (!VALID_VIDEO_TYPES.contains(mimeType)) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Invalid video type", mimeType, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+        }
+
+        StreamKind streamKind = detectStreamKind(path);
+
+        if (streamKind == StreamKind.AUDIO_ONLY) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(true, null, mimeType, MediaKind.AUDIO);
+        }
+
+        if (streamKind == StreamKind.VIDEO_ONLY || streamKind == StreamKind.AUDIO_VIDEO) {
+            return new MediaAnalysis(true, null, mimeType, MediaKind.VIDEO);
+        }
+
+        return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Invalid video content", mimeType, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+    }
+
+    public MediaAnalysis analyzePostFile(Path path) throws Exception {
+
+        String mimeType = tika.detect(path);
+        if (VALID_IMAGE_TYPES.contains(mimeType)) {
+            return analyzeImage(path, mimeType);
+        }
+
+        if (VALID_VIDEO_TYPES.contains(mimeType)) {
+            return analyzeVideo(path, mimeType);
+
+        }
+        return new MediaAnalysis(false, "Invalid file type", mimeType, MediaKind.UNKNOWN);
+    }
+
+    private StreamKind detectStreamKind(Path path) throws Exception {
+
+        ProcessBuilder pb = new ProcessBuilder(
+          "ffprobe",
+          "-v", "error",
+          "-show_entries", "stream=codec_type",
+          "-of", "json",
+          path.toAbsolutePath().toString()
+        );
+        pb.redirectErrorStream(true);
+
+
+        Process process = pb.start();
+        process.getOutputStream().close();
+
+        boolean finished = process.waitFor(3, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+
+        if (!finished) {
+            process.destroyForcibly();
+            System.out.println("ffprobe timeout");
+            return StreamKind.UNKNOWN;
+        }
+
+        String output = new String(process.getInputStream().readAllBytes());
+        int exit = process.exitValue();
+
+
+        if (exit != 0) {
+            return StreamKind.UNKNOWN;
+        }
+
+        JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(output);
+        JsonNode streams = root.path("streams");
+
+        boolean hasAudio = false;
+        boolean hasVideo = false;
+
+        for (JsonNode stream : streams) {
+            String codecType = stream.path("codec_type").asText();
+
+            if ("audio".equals(codecType))
+                hasAudio = true;
+            if ("video".equals(codecType))
+                hasVideo = true;
+        }
+
+        if (hasAudio && hasVideo) return StreamKind.AUDIO_VIDEO;
+        if (hasVideo) return StreamKind.VIDEO_ONLY;
+        if (hasAudio) return StreamKind.AUDIO_ONLY;
+        return StreamKind.UNKNOWN;
+    }
+
+    private enum StreamKind {
+        AUDIO_ONLY,
+        VIDEO_ONLY,
+        AUDIO_VIDEO,
+        UNKNOWN
+    }
+
+    public boolean isValidImage(Path path) {
+        try (InputStream is = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
+            BufferedImage img = ImageIO.read(is);
+            return img != null;
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/storage/MinioStorageService.java b/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/storage/MinioStorageService.java
new file mode 100644

+++ b/backend/src/main/java/com/ft/transcendence/common/storage/MinioStorageService.java
@@ -0,0 +1,168 @@
+package com.ft.transcendence.common.storage;
+
+import io.minio.*;
+import io.minio.errors.ErrorResponseException;
+import io.minio.http.Method;
+import io.minio.messages.Item;
+import io.quarkus.runtime.StartupEvent;
+import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
+import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
+import jakarta.inject.Inject;
+import org.eclipse.microprofile.config.inject.ConfigProperty;
+
+import java.io.InputStream;
+
+@ApplicationScoped
+public class MinioStorageService implements StorageService {
+
+    @Inject
+    private MinioClient minioClient;
+
+    @ConfigProperty(name = "quarkus.minio.bucket-name")
+    private String bucket;
+
+    void onStart(@Observes StartupEvent ev) {
+        // Criar bucket, depois remover isso e colocar pra criar o bucket por propeties
+        try {
+            boolean found = minioClient.bucketExists(
+                    BucketExistsArgs.builder().bucket(bucket).build()
+            );
+
+            if (!found) {
+                minioClient.makeBucket(
+                        MakeBucketArgs.builder().bucket(bucket).build()
+                );
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            System.err.println("Erro ao checar/criado bucket: " + e.getMessage());
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public String upload(String path, InputStream stream, long size, String contentType) {
+        try {
+            minioClient.putObject(
+                    PutObjectArgs.builder()
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .stream(stream, size, -1)
+                            .contentType(contentType)
+                            .build()
+            );
+            return path;
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public InputStream download(String path) {
+        try {
+            // Download the file from MinIO
+            InputStream stream = minioClient.getObject( //(2)
+                    GetObjectArgs.builder()
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .build()
+            );
+            return stream;
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public InputStream view(String path) {
+        try {
+            // 1. Pergunta ao MinIO os metadados do arquivo (para descobrir se é png, jpg, etc)
+            StatObjectResponse stat = minioClient.statObject(
+                    StatObjectArgs.builder()
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .build()
+            );
+
+            // 2. Pega o fluxo de dados (a imagem em si)
+            InputStream stream = minioClient.getObject(
+                    GetObjectArgs.builder()
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .build()
+            );
+
+            // 3. Retorna o stream com o Tipo de Conteúdo correto e a disposição "inline"
+            return stream;
+
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void delete(String path) {
+        try {
+            minioClient.removeObject(
+                    RemoveObjectArgs.builder()
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .build()
+            );
+        } catch (ErrorResponseException e) {
+            if (e.errorResponse().code().equals("NoSuchKey")) {
+                return; // arquivo não existe
+            }
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        }
+
+    }
+
+    // users/1/posts/10/images/b.png
+    // deleteByPrefix("users/1/posts/10/");
+    // deleteByPrefix("users/1/posts/10/comments/5/");
+    // deleteByPrefix("users/{userId}/posts/{postId}/");
+    @Override
+    public void deleteByPrefix(String prefix) {
+        Iterable<Result<Item>> results = minioClient.listObjects(
+                ListObjectsArgs.builder()
+                        .bucket(bucket)
+                        .prefix(prefix)
+                        .recursive(true)
+                        .build()
+        );
+
+        for (Result<Item> result : results) {
+            try {
+                Item item = result.get();
+
+                minioClient.removeObject(
+                        RemoveObjectArgs.builder()
+                                .bucket(bucket)
+                                .object(item.objectName())
+                                .build()
+                );
+
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                throw new RuntimeException(e);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public String generateUrl(String path) {
+        try {
+            String url = minioClient.getPresignedObjectUrl(
+                    GetPresignedObjectUrlArgs.builder()
+                            .method(Method.GET)
+                            .bucket(bucket)
+                            .object(path)
+                            .expiry(60 * 60) // 1 hora
+                            .build()
+            );
+            return url;
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new RuntimeException(e);
+        }
+    }

+package com.ft.transcendence.post.app;
+
+import com.ft.transcendence.common.media.MediaAnalysis;
+import com.ft.transcendence.common.media.MediaValidationService;
+import com.ft.transcendence.common.storage.StorageService;
+import com.ft.transcendence.like.domain.Like;
+import com.ft.transcendence.post.domain.Post;
+import com.ft.transcendence.post.dto.CreatePostForm;
+import com.ft.transcendence.post.dto.FeedResponse;
+import com.ft.transcendence.post.dto.PostResponse;
+import com.ft.transcendence.user.domain.User;
+import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
+import jakarta.inject.Inject;
+import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
+import jakarta.ws.rs.BadRequestException;
+
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.io.InputStream;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
+import java.util.HashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+@ApplicationScoped
+public class PostService {
+
+    @Inject
+    StorageService storageService;
+
+    @Inject
+    MediaValidationService mediaValidationService;
+
+    @Transactional
+    public PostResponse create(Long userId, CreatePostForm req) throws IOException {
+        if ((req.text == null || req.text.isBlank()) && (req.file == null))
+            throw new BadRequestException("Posts must contain text or images");
+
+        // file
+        String contentType = null;
+        long size = 0;
+        MediaAnalysis analysis;
+        if (req.file != null) {
+            Path uploadedPath = req.file.uploadedFile();
+            size = req.file.size();
+
+            try {
+                analysis = mediaValidationService.analyzePostFile(uploadedPath);
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                throw new BadRequestException("Error validating file");
+            }
+
+            if (!analysis.valid()) {
+                throw new BadRequestException(analysis.error());
+            }
+
+            contentType = analysis.detectedMimeType();
+        }
+
+        var now = OffsetDateTime.now();
+
+        Post post = new Post();
+        post.user = User.findById(userId);
+        post.text = (req.text == null || req.text.isBlank()) ? null : req.text;
+        post.mediaUrl = null;
+        post.likeCount = 0L;
+        post.commentCount = 0L;
+        post.createdAt = now;
+        post.updatedAt = now;
+        post.persist();
+
+        String mediaUrl = null;
+
+        if (req.file != null) {
+            String originalName = req.file.fileName();
+            String extension = originalName.substring(originalName.lastIndexOf("."));
+            String fullNameFile = java.util.UUID.randomUUID() + extension;
+            String path = "users/" + userId + "/post/" + post.id + "/" + fullNameFile;
+
+            try (InputStream stream = Files.newInputStream(req.file.uploadedFile())) {
+                storageService.upload(path, stream, size, contentType);
+            }
+
+            post.mediaUrl = path;
+            mediaUrl = path;
+
+            if (mediaUrl != null && !mediaUrl.isBlank() && !(mediaUrl.contains("http") || mediaUrl.contains("blob"))) {
+                mediaUrl = storageService.generateUrl(mediaUrl);
+            }
+        }
+
+
+        String avatarUrl  = post.user.avatarUrl;
+        if (avatarUrl != null) {
+            avatarUrl = storageService.generateUrl(avatarUrl);
+        }
+
+        return PostResponse.of(post, false, avatarUrl, mediaUrl);
+    }
+
+    public PostResponse getPostById(Long postId, Long currentUserId) {
+        Post post = Post.findById(postId);
+        boolean likedByMe = Like.find("user.id = ?1 and post.id = ?2", currentUserId, postId).firstResultOptional().isPresent();
+
+        String mediaUrl = post.mediaUrl;
+
+        if (mediaUrl != null && !mediaUrl.isBlank() && !(mediaUrl.contains("http") || mediaUrl.contains("blob"))) {
+            mediaUrl = storageService.generateUrl(mediaUrl);
+        }
+
+        String avatarUrl  = post.user.avatarUrl;
+        if (avatarUrl != null) {
+            avatarUrl = storageService.generateUrl(avatarUrl);
+        }
+        return PostResponse.of(post, likedByMe, avatarUrl, mediaUrl);
+    }
+
+    public FeedResponse feed(int page, int size, long userId) {
+        if (page < 0 || size <= 0)
+            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid page or size");
+
+        int start = page * size;
+        int end = start + size;
+
+        List<Post> posts = Post.find("order by createdAt desc")
+                .range(start, end).list();
+
+        boolean hasMore = posts.size() > size;
+        if (hasMore)
+            posts = posts.subList(0, size);
+
+        List<Long> postIds = posts.stream().map(p -> p.id).toList();
+
+        List<Like> likes = Like.find("user.id = ?1 and post.id in ?2", userId, postIds).list();
+
+        var likedPostIds = likes.stream().map(l -> l.post.id).collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toSet());
+
+        FeedResponse resp = new FeedResponse();
+
+
+        Map<String, String> avatarCache = new HashMap<>();
+        resp.items = posts.stream().map(p -> {
+
+            String mediaUrl = p.mediaUrl;
+
+            if (mediaUrl != null && !mediaUrl.isBlank() && !(mediaUrl.contains("http") || mediaUrl.contains("blob"))) {
+                mediaUrl = storageService.generateUrl(mediaUrl);
+            }
+
+            String avatarUrl  = p.user.avatarUrl;
+            if (avatarUrl != null) {
+                avatarUrl = avatarCache.computeIfAbsent(
+                        avatarUrl,
+                        storageService::generateUrl
+                );
+            }
+
+            return  PostResponse.of(p, likedPostIds.contains(p.id), avatarUrl, mediaUrl);
+        }).toList();
+
+        resp.page = page;
+        resp.size = size;
+        resp.hasMore = hasMore;
+        return resp;
+    }
+
+
+}

+
+    @Transactional
+    public Response updateAvatar(long userId, FileUploadForm req) throws IOException {
+
+        if (req.file == null)
+            throw new BadRequestException("Avatar upload must contain image");
+
+
+
+        String contentType = req.file.contentType();
+        MediaAnalysis analysis;
+        Path uploadedPath = req.file.uploadedFile();
+        try {
+            analysis = mediaValidationService.analyzeImage(uploadedPath);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            throw new BadRequestException("Error validating file");
+        }
+
+        if (!analysis.valid()) {
+            throw new BadRequestException(analysis.error());
+        }
+
+
+        String mimeType = analysis.detectedMimeType();
+
+        String extension = switch (mimeType) {
+            case "image/jpeg" -> ".jpg";
+            case "image/png" -> ".png";
+            case "image/webp" -> ".webp";
+            case "image/gif" -> ".gif";
+            default -> ".png";
+        };
+        String path = "users/" + userId + "/avatar/avatar" + extension;
+
+        InputStream stream = Files.newInputStream(req.file.uploadedFile());
+        long size = req.file.size();
+
+        storageService.upload(path, stream, size, mimeType);
+        User user = User.findById(userId);
+        user.avatarUrl = path;
+        String avatarUrl = storageService.generateUrl(path);
+        return Response.ok(
+                new UpdateAvatarResponse(avatarUrl)
+        ).build();
+    }
 }

diff --git a/frontend/src/design-system/primitives/Avatar.jsx b/frontend/src/design-system/primitives/Avatar.jsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2b3d91d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/design-system/primitives/Avatar.jsx
@@ -0,0 +1,110 @@
+/** Maybe appear in Feed, Chat, Amigos, Perfil, Sidebar
+ * size="sm | md | lg" online={true} src="..." fallback="MS"
+ */
+import { useState } from 'react'
+import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
+
+function getInitials(name = ""){
+	const names = name.trim().split(" ");
+	if (names.length === 1)
+		return names[0][0]?.toUpperCase();
+	return(
+		(names[0][0] || "") +
+		(names[names.length - 1][0] || "")
+	).toUpperCase();
+}
+
+function Avatar ({src, name, username, size="md", className="", ...props}){
+	const [hasError, setHasError] = useState(false);
+
+	const sizes = {
+		sm: "h-8 w-8 text-xs",
+		md: "h-10 w-10 text-sm",
+		lg: "h-14 w-14 text-lg",
+		xl: "h-20 w-20 text-xl",
+		xl2: "h-24 w-24 text-2xl",
+		xl3: "h-28 w-28 text-3xl",
+		xxl: "h-32 w-32 text-4xl"
+	};
+
+	const dimension = sizes[size] || sizes.md;
+
+	const ModalAvatar = (
+		<div
+			className={`relative flex items-center justify-center border
+			rounded-full text-
+			bg-gradient-to-r from-[#6e93c4] via-[#4a78b5] to-[#3b6091]  shadow-[#4a78b5]/50 h-9
+			font-medium overflow-hidden
+			${dimension}
+			${className}
+			`}
+			>
+			{src && !hasError ? (
+				<img
+					src={src}
+					alt={name}
+					onError={() => setHasError(true)}
+					className="h-full w-full object-cover block"
+					{...props}
+				/>
+			):(
+				<span>{src || getInitials(name)}</span>
+			)}
+		</div>
+	)
+
+	if (username) {
+		return(
+			<Link to={`/${username}`}>
+				{ModalAvatar}
+			</Link>
+		);
+	} else {
+		return(
+			<>
+				{ModalAvatar}
+			</>
+		);
+	}
+
+}
+
+
+function AvatarWithStatus({
+  src,
+  name,
+  size = "md",
+  status = "online", // online | offline | busy
+  className = "",
+}) {
+  const statusColors = {
+    online: "bg-green-500",
+    offline: "bg-gray-400",
+    busy: "bg-red-500",
+  };
+
+  const statusSizes = {
+    sm: "h-2.5 w-2.5",
+    md: "h-3 w-3",
+    lg: "h-4 w-4",
+    xl: "h-5 w-5",
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className={`relative inline-block ${className}`}>
+      <Avatar src={src} name={name} size={size} />
+
+      <span
+        className={`
+          absolute bottom-0 right-0
+          rounded-full border-2 border-white
+          ${statusColors[status]}
+          ${statusSizes[size]}
+        `}
+      />
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+
+export { Avatar, AvatarWithStatus };

+import React, { useState } from 'react'
+import Cropper from 'react-easy-crop'
+import { Button } from '@/design-system'
+import { getCroppedImage } from '@/features/profile/utils/imageCrop'
+
+// // Avatar — círculo, 1:1
+// <CroppedImage
+//   cropShape="round"
+//   aspect={1}
+//   onSave={handleAvatarSave}
+// />
+
+// // Cover — retângulo, 3:1, zoom livre
+// <CroppedImage
+//   cropShape="rect"
+//   aspect={3 / 1}
+//   onSave={handleCoverSave}
+// />
+
+export const CroppedImage = ({ image, cropShape = 'rect', aspect = 1, setNewImage, onClose, ...props }) => {
+
+    const [crop, onCropChange] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 })
+    const [zoom, onZoomChange] = useState(1);
+    const [croppedPixel, setCroppedPixel] = useState(null);
+
+    const onCropComplete = (croppedArea, croppedAreaPixels) => {
+        setCroppedPixel(croppedAreaPixels);
+    }
+
+    const onSave = async () => {
+
+        try {
+            
+            const { url, file } = await getCroppedImage(image, croppedPixel);
+            setNewImage(file);
+        } finally {
+
+            onClose(null);
+        }
+    }
+
+    return (
+        <div {...props} className='flex  flex-col w-full h-full bg-card border border-border rounded'>
+            <div className="relative flex-1 rounded">
+                <Cropper
+                    image={image}
+                    crop={crop}
+                    zoom={zoom}
+                    cropShape={cropShape}
+                    aspect={aspect}
+                    onCropChange={onCropChange}
+                    onZoomChange={onZoomChange}
+                    onCropComplete={onCropComplete}
+                />
+
+            </div>
+            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-8 p-4 h-16 shrink-0 bg-card/50">
+                <input
+                    type="range"
+                    value={zoom}
+                    min={1}
+                    max={3}
+                    step={0.1}
+                    aria-labelledby="Zoom"
+                    onChange={(e) => {
+                        onZoomChange(Number(e.target.value))
+                    }}
+                    className="w-1/2 cursor-pointer"
+                />
+                <Button onClick={() => onSave()}>
+                    Salvar
+                </Button>
+            </div>
+
+        </div>
+    )
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/components/lists/ProfilePostList.jsx b/frontend/src/features/profile/components/lists/ProfilePostList.jsx

index 0000000..5e043f0

@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
+import { PostListUI } from "@/features/profile/components/lists/PostListUI";
+import { getUserPosts } from "@/features/profile/services/profileService";
+import { usePostList } from "@/features/profile/hooks/usePostList";
+import { useProfile } from "@/features/profile/context/ProfileContext";
+import { Loading } from "@/design-system";
+import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
+
+export function ProfilePostList({ username }) {
+
+
+  const { posts, loadInitial, setPosts, ...handlers } = usePostList(
+    (page, size) => getUserPosts(username, page, size)
+  );
+  const { userData } = useProfile();
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+
+    const getUserPosts = async () => {
+      try {
+        await loadInitial();
+
+      } catch (err) {
+
+        console.error(err);
+        toast.error("Falha ao buscar posts do usuário");
+      } finally {
+        setLoading(false);
+      }
+    }
+
+    getUserPosts();
+  }, [username]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setPosts(prev =>
+      prev.map(post =>
+        post.userName === userData.username
+          ? {
+            ...post,
+            avatarUrl: userData.avatarUrl 
+          }
+          : post
+      )
+    );
+  }, [userData.avatarUrl]);
+
+  if (loading)
+    return <Loading size="md" className="py-10" />
+  return <PostListUI posts={posts} {...handlers} />;
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/components/modals/FollowersModal.jsx b/frontend/src/features/profile/components/modals/FollowersModal.jsx

@@ -0,0 +1,215 @@
+// components/FollowersModal.jsx
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { followUser, unfollowUser, getFollowersList, getFollowingList } from '@/features/profile/services/profileService';
+import { Button, Scroll, Avatar, Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger, TabsContent, Modal, Loading } from '@/design-system';
+import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
+
+function UserList({ users, loading, currentUserId, onClose, toggleFollow, changeFollowCount }) {
+
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+
+  function handleNavigate(username) {
+    onClose?.();
+    navigate(`/${username}`);
+  }
+
+  const handleFollowUser = async (username) => {
+
+    try {
+      await followUser(username);
+      toggleFollow(username);
+      changeFollowCount('follow');
+    } catch (err) {
+      console.error(err)
+    } finally {
+
+    }
+  }
+
+  const handleUnfollowUser = async (username) => {
+
+    try {
+      await unfollowUser(username);
+      toggleFollow(username);
+      changeFollowCount('unfollow');
+    } catch (err) {
+      console.error(err)
+    } finally {
+
+    }
+  }
+
+  const handleUserFollowrs = async (username, isFollowing) => {
+
+    if (isFollowing) {
+      await handleUnfollowUser(username);
+    } else {
+      await handleFollowUser(username);
+    }
+
+  }
+
+  if (loading) {
+    return (
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-10">
+        <Loading />
+      </div>
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (!users.length) {
+    return (
+      <p className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-10 text-sm">
+        Nenhum resultado ainda.
+      </p>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Scroll className="divide-y divide-border max-h-[50vh] overflow-y-auto">
+      {users.map((user, index) => (
+        <span
+          key={`${user.id}-${index}`}
+          className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-accent transition-colors"
+        >
+          <button onClick={() => onClose?.()}>
+            <Avatar
+              src={user?.avatarUrl ?? null}
+              alt={user.name}
+              name={user.name}
+              username={user.username}
+              size='md'
+            />
+          </button>
+          <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-w-0">
+            <Link
+              to={`/${user.username}`}
+              onClick={() => handleNavigate(user.username)}
+              className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground
+                         hover:text-primary transition-colors truncate"
+            >
+              {user.name}
+            </Link>
+            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">
+              @{user.username}
+            </span>
+          </div>
+
+          {user?.isFollowing &&
+            <>
+              {
+                user.id !== currentUserId && (
+                  <Button
+                    variant={user.isFollowing ? "secondary" : "primary"}
+                    size="follow"
+                    onClick={() => handleUserFollowrs(user.username, user.isFollowing)}
+                  >
+                    {user.isFollowing ? 'Seguindo' : 'Seguir'}
+                  </Button>
+                )
+              }
+            </>
+          }
+        </span>
+      ))}
+    </Scroll>
+  );
+}
+
+export function FollowersModal({ open, onClose, defaultTab = 'followers', userId, currentUserId, username, changeFollowCount }) {
+
+  const [followers, setFollowers] = useState([]);
+  const [following, setFollowing] = useState([]);
+  const [loadingFollowers, setLoadingFollowers] = useState(false);
+  const [loadingFollowing, setLoadingFollowing] = useState(false);
+  const { t } = useTranslation('profile');
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!open || !userId) return;
+
+    const fetchFollowers = async () => {
+      setLoadingFollowers(true);
+      try {
+        const res = await getFollowersList(username);
+        setFollowers(res.items);
+      } finally {
+        setLoadingFollowers(false);
+      }
+    };
+
+    const fetchFollowing = async () => {
+
+      setLoadingFollowing(true);
+      try {
+        const res = await getFollowingList(username);
+        setFollowing(res.items);
+      } finally {
+        setLoadingFollowing(false);
+      }
+    };
+
+    // Busca os dois em paralelo ao abrir
+    fetchFollowers();
+    fetchFollowing();
+  }, [open, username]);
+
+  const toggleFollow = (username) => {
+    setFollowers((prevFollowers) => {
+      const updated = prevFollowers.map((user) =>
+        user.username === username
+          ? { ...user, isFollowing: !user.isFollowing }
+          : user
+      );
+
+      const targetUser = updated.find((u) => u.username === username);
+
+      setFollowing((prevFollowing) => {
+        if (targetUser.isFollowing) {
+          return [...prevFollowing, targetUser];
+        } else {
+          return prevFollowing.filter((u) => u.username !== username);
+        }
+      });
+
+      return updated;
+    });
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={t('info.followersModal.title')} size="md" scrollable={false}>
+      <Tabs defaultValue={defaultTab} className="-mt-2 p-2">
+        <TabsList className="w-full mb-2">
+          <TabsTrigger value="following" className="flex-1">
+            {t('info.following')}
+          </TabsTrigger>
+          <TabsTrigger value="followers" className="flex-1">
+            {t('info.followers')}
+          </TabsTrigger>
+        </TabsList>
+
+        <TabsContent value="following">
+          <UserList
+            users={following}
+            loading={loadingFollowing}
+            currentUserId={currentUserId}
+            onClose={onClose}
+            toggleFollow={toggleFollow}
+            changeFollowCount={changeFollowCount}
+          />
+        </TabsContent>
+        <TabsContent value="followers">
+          <UserList
+            users={followers}
+            loading={loadingFollowers}
+            currentUserId={currentUserId}
+            onClose={onClose}
+            toggleFollow={toggleFollow}
+            changeFollowCount={changeFollowCount}
+          />
+        </TabsContent>
+
+      </Tabs>
+    </Modal>
+  );
+}

+import React, { useEffect } from 'react'
+
+
+export const ImageCropModal = ({ children, onClose }) => {
+
+    useEffect(() => {
+        const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
+            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+                onClose();
+            }
+        };
+
+        window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
+
+        return () => {
+            window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
+        };
+    }, [onClose]);
+
+    return (
+        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50
+            flex items-center justify-center p-1 sm:p-4
+            bg-back/40
+            backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
+            <div className="fixed inset-x-1/12 inset-y-1/6 sm:inset-1/6 z-10" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
+                {children}
+                <button
+                    onClick={onClose}
+                    className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-50 bg-black/50 text-white rounded-full w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-black/70 cursor-pointer"
+                >
+                    X
+                </button>
+            </div>
+        </div>
+    )
+}

+import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
+import { Avatar } from "@/design-system";
+import { FileUploadButton } from '@/design-system';
+import { cn } from '@/design-system/tokens/colors';
+
+
+export const AvatarEditable = ({ user, canEdit = false, onChange }) => {
+
+    const [showHint, setShowHint] = useState(true);
+
+
+    useEffect(() => {
+        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+            setShowHint(false);
+        }, 1000);
+
+        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
+    }, []);
+
+    return (
+        <div className="-mt-16 rounded-full p-1 relative z-5 border-background bg-background">
+            <div className="relative group">
+                <Avatar
+                    size="xl3"
+                    id={user?.userName}
+                    name={user?.name}
+                    src={user?.avatarUrl}
+                    className={canEdit ? "group-hover:brightness-75" : ""}
+                />
+
+                {canEdit && (
+                    <label className={cn(
+                        "absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center",
+                        "transition-opacity duration-1000",
+                        showHint
+                            ? "opacity-100"
+                            : "opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
+                    )}
+                    >
+
+                        <FileUploadButton
+                            onFileSelect={onChange}
+                            className="text-accent-foreground text-xs font-bold bg-accent/60 px-2 py-1 rounded cursor-pointer"
+                            type="file"
+                            accept=".jpg,.png"
+                            maxMBSize={5}
+                        />
+                    </label>
+                )}
+            </div>
+        </div>
+    )
+}

+

+

+
+		try {
+			await followUser(username);
+			changeFields({ isFollowing: true });
+			changeFollowCount('follow');
+		} catch (err) {
+			console.error(err);
+			toast.error("Falha ao seguir")
+		} finally {
+
+		}
+	}
+
+	const handleUnfollowUser = async (username) => {
+
+		try {
+			await unfollowUser(username);
+			changeFields({ isFollowing: false });
+			changeFollowCount('unfollow');
+		} catch (err) {
+			console.error(err)
+			toast.error("Falha ao deixar de seguir")
+		} finally {
+
+		}
+	}
+
+	const handleUserFollowrs = async (username, isFollowing) => {
+
+		if (isFollowing) {
+			await handleUnfollowUser(username);

+			await handleFollowUser(username);
+		}
+
+	}

+

+		if (!typeValidation.valid) {
+			toast.error(contentValidation.error);
+			return;
+		}
+
+		const contentValidation = await validateImageContent(file);
+		if (!contentValidation.valid) {
+			toast.error(contentValidation.error);
+			return;
+		}
+
+		const imageUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
+		setNewImage(imageUrl);
+	}
+
+	const setNewUrlImage = (image) => {
+
+		changeFields({ avatarUrl: image })
+	}
+
+	const openFollowers = () => setConnectionsModal({ open: true, tab: 'followers' });
+	const openFollowing = () => setConnectionsModal({ open: true, tab: 'following' });

+	const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/components/ui/midiaThumbnails/MediaGridRenderer.jsx b/frontend/src/features/profile/components/ui/midiaThumbnails/MediaGridRenderer.jsx

+++ b/frontend/src/features/profile/components/ui/midiaThumbnails/MediaGridRenderer.jsx
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+import React from 'react'
+import { VideoThumbnail } from './VideoThumbnail';
+import { AudioThumbnail } from './AudioThumbnail';
+import { UnknownMedia } from './UnknownMedia';
+import { resolveMediaTypeSync } from '@/shared/resolveMediaType';
+
+export const MediaGridRenderer = ({ item }) => {
+    
+    const type = resolveMediaTypeSync(item?.mediaUrl);
+
+    if (type === "image") {
+        return (
+            <img
+                src={item.mediaUrl}
+                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
+            />
+        );
+    }
+
+    if (type === "video") {
+        return (
+            <VideoThumbnail thumbnailUrl={item.mediaUrl} />
+        );
+    }
+
+    if (type === "audio") {
+        return <AudioThumbnail />;
+    }
+
+    return <UnknownMedia />;
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/components/ui/midiaThumbnails/VideoThumbnail.jsx b/frontend/src/features/profile/components/ui/midiaThumbnails/VideoThumbnail.jsx

+import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
+import { generateVideoThumbnailFromUrl } from "@/features/profile/utils/generateVideoThumbnail";
+import { Loading } from "@/design-system";
+import { Play } from 'lucide-react'
+
+
+export const VideoThumbnail = ({ thumbnailUrl }) => {
+
+    const [thumbnail, setThumbnail] = useState(null);
+    const [error, setError] = useState(false);
+
+    useEffect(() => {
+
+        const getThumbnail = async () => {
+
+            try {
+                const image = await generateVideoThumbnailFromUrl(thumbnailUrl);
+
+                if (!image) {
+                    setThumbnail("https://t4.ftcdn.net/jpg/05/24/04/51/360_F_524045110_UXnCx4GEDapddDi5tdlY96s4g0MxHRvt.jpg")
+                    setError(true);
+                    return;
+                }
+                const imageUrl = URL.createObjectURL(image);
+                setThumbnail(imageUrl);
+            } catch (err) {
+                setThumbnail("https://t4.ftcdn.net/jpg/05/24/04/51/360_F_524045110_UXnCx4GEDapddDi5tdlY96s4g0MxHRvt.jpg")
+                setError(true);
+                console.log(err);
+            }
+        }
+
+        getThumbnail();
+    }, [thumbnailUrl]);
+
+
+    if (thumbnail == null) {
+        return (
+            <Loading size="sm" />
+        );
+    }
+
+    return (
+        <div className="relative w-full h-full cursor-pointer group">
+
+            <img
+                src={thumbnail}
+                alt="video thumbnail"
+                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
+                onError={(e) => {
+                    e.currentTarget.src = `${error}`;
+                }}
+            />
+
+            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/20 group-hover:bg-black/30 transition" />
+
+            {!error &&
+                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
+                    <div className="size-12 sm:size-15 rounded-full bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center">
+                        <Play />
+                    </div>
+                </div>
+            }
+        </div>
+    );
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/generateVideoThumbnail.js b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/generateVideoThumbnail.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e21122e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/generateVideoThumbnail.js
@@ -0,0 +1,59 @@
+
+
+export function generateVideoThumbnailFromFile(file) {
+
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+        const video = document.createElement("video");
+        video.src = URL.createObjectURL(file);
+        video.currentTime = 1; // pega frame em 1s
+
+        video.onloadeddata = () => {
+            const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
+            canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
+            canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
+
+            const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
+            ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
+
+            canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
+                resolve(blob);
+            }, "image/jpeg", 0.8);
+        };
+    });
+}
+
+export function generateVideoThumbnailFromUrl(url) {
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+        const video = document.createElement("video");
+        video.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
+        video.src = url;
+
+        video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
+            if (video.videoWidth === 0 || video.videoHeight === 0) {
+                return resolve(null);
+            }
+
+            video.currentTime = 1;
+        };
+
+        video.onseeked = () => {
+            try {
+                const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
+                canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
+                canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
+
+                const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
+                ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
+
+                canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
+                    resolve(blob || null);
+                }, "image/jpeg", 0.6);
+
+            } catch (err) {
+                resolve(null);
+            }
+        };
+
+        video.onerror = () => resolve(null);
+    });
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/imageCrop.js b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/imageCrop.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3e7fca4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/imageCrop.js
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+
+// Cria elemento Image a partir de uma URL
+export const createImage = (url) => {
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+        const image = new Image();
+        image.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(image));
+        image.addEventListener('error', reject);
+        image.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
+        image.src = url;
+    });
+};
+
+// Recebe src + pixelCrop, retorna object URL da imagem cortada
+export const getCroppedImage = async (imageSrc, pixelCrop, format = 'image/jpeg', quality = 0.8) => {
+    const image = await createImage(imageSrc);
+
+    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
+    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
+
+    if (!ctx) {
+        throw new Error("fail to cropped");
+    }
+
+    canvas.width = pixelCrop.width;
+    canvas.height = pixelCrop.height;
+
+    //  drawImage(img, sx, sy, sWidth, sHeight, dx, dy, dWidth, dHeight)
+    //  sx, sy, sWidth, sHeight
+    //  de onde cortar na imagem original
+
+    //  dx, dy, dWidth, dHeight
+    //  onde desenhar no canvas
+    ctx.drawImage(
+        image,
+        pixelCrop.x,
+        pixelCrop.y,
+        pixelCrop.width,
+        pixelCrop.height,
+        0, 0,
+        pixelCrop.width,
+        pixelCrop.height
+    );
+
+    // Blob = Binary Large Object // um arquivo em memória (imagem, vídeo, etc)
+    // Pode liberar depois com URL.revokeObjectURL(file || url)
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+        canvas.toBlob((file) => {
+            if (!file) {
+                return reject(new Error('Error create image'));
+            }
+
+            resolve({
+                url: URL.createObjectURL(file),
+                file: file
+            });
+        }, format, quality);
+    });
+};

diff --git a/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/validFile.js b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/validFile.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6e6909b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/features/profile/utils/validFile.js
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+import i18n from "@/i18n";
+
+const validImageTypes = [
+    "image/jpeg",
+    "image/png",
+    "image/webp",
+    "image/gif"
+]
+
+const validVideoTypes = [
+    "video/mp4"
+]
+
+const limits = {
+    avatar: 2 * 1024 * 1024, // 2MB
+    image: 5 * 1024 * 1024,  // 5MB
+    video: 20 * 1024 * 1024  // 20MB
+};
+
+export function validImageFile(file) {
+
+    if (!file) {
+        return { valid: false, error: i18n.t("errors.upload.notFound") };
+    }
+
+    if (!validImageTypes.includes(file.type)) {
+        return { valid: false, error: i18n.t("errors.upload.invalidType") };
+    }
+
+    return { valid: true };
+}
+
+export function validVideoFile(file) {
+
+    if (!file) {
+        return { valid: false, error: i18n.t("errors.upload.notFound") };
+    }
+
+    if (!validVideoTypes.includes(file.type)) {
+        return { valid: false, error: i18n.t("errors.upload.invalidType") };
+    }
+
+    return { valid: true };
+
+}
+
+export function validateImageContent(file) {
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+        const img = new Image();
+        const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
+
+        img.onload = () => {
+            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
+            resolve({ valid: true });
+        };
+
+        img.onerror = () => {
+            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
+            resolve({ valid: false, error: i18n.t("errors.upload.invalidImage") });
+        };
+
+        img.src = url;
+    });
+}

+  return '';
+}
+
+export function resolveMediaTypeSync(url) {
+  const cleanUrl = url.split('?')[0];
+  const extension = cleanUrl.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
+
+  if (['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'].includes(extension)) return 'video';
+  if (['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'].includes(extension)) return 'image';
+
   return '';
 }
